## Selection, projection and capability

One `ColdSelection` has `tenant_id,site_id,code,from_at,to_at,known_at,watermark,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,assessment_revision_id,finding_revision_ids,source_gate`. `from_at` and `to_at` are UTC and half-open; `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark captured once at request start. Omitted `from,to` selects W2-03's latest settled cold window; exactly one omitted is 422. The effective Foundation/binding is selected at each evidence source time (W2-03), with the selected policy at the requested window; conflicting/absent effective revision is an explicit limitation. 31 days maximum. No newer assessment, Finding or consequence may be silently mixed with an older `known_at`. A late accepted report makes the current projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new exact assessment and cold consequence both settle. A selected old cutoff is `HISTORICAL`, even when there is no pending work. Determine probe freshness from the latest qualifying accepted probe `source_time` within `[from,to)` compared with `to`: over 10 minutes is `STALE_SOURCE`. Later wall-clock viewing of the 2026-10-06 replay never alone makes it stale or live. Precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit cutoff older than latest relevant committed disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection/probe or future `to` -> `UNKNOWN`; old last probe -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. Money can remain `UNPRICED` within a current technical read; pending/old/unresolved money has its own withholding limit.

`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal string}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` follows the commit-3 ruling below. `fact_age` is a nonnegative integer number of seconds/null between selected `to` and the last accepted source fact in `[from,to)` at the cutoff; it is distinct from contact. No source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's â€œnext evidenceâ€ is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.

**Commit-3 Architect ruling â€” source contact.** Select the latest registered-gateway diagnostic received no later than both `known_at` and selected `to` in the same Site snapshot. Only its explicit `last_device_contact_by_source[source_id]` entry can establish that source's contact; gateway receipt alone cannot. A nonnull source contact instant at or before selected `to`, with a trusted gateway clock, is `RECENT` when `to - contact_at <= 2 * gateway.declared_cadence_seconds`, otherwise `STALE`. Equality is recent. This is the S7 gateway freshness multiplier, applied to the source-reported contact instant and W2-03's selected window end, not request wall time or diagnostic receipt time. An explicit null source entry is `NEVER` with null contact time: the diagnostic reports no recorded source contact. No selected diagnostic, a diagnostic without this source key, missing/unusable cadence, untrusted or unverifiable reported time, or contact time after `to` is `UNKNOWN` with a specific limitation and null claim of recency. A diagnostic read failure remains a typed unavailable read, never a successful `NEVER` or `UNKNOWN` projection. The W2-01 cold null-to-`NEVER` ruling and W2-05a EV ruling concern **gateway contact**, whose receipt freshness is checked at request time for Foundation/Readiness; they do not turn absent per-source history into `NEVER`. Cold and EV use this same gateway-versus-source distinction. On cold Evidence panel 2 use `Recent source contact`, `Last source contact is old`, `No source contact recorded`, and `Source contact unknown` respectively, with `Contact checked at window end {to}` and a separate `Last accepted fact` age. Do not call any state device health or live reporting.

Analysis rows are selected accepted public records from the pinned assessment basis and exact links, ordered `(source_time,evidence_id)`, with `evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id`; `exclusion` is W2-03's SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED reason or null. Keep a record in the list when excluded, label why, and link to the existing Site Evidence exact record when source gate permits. `claim_rules` lists rule ID `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1`, requirement, observed/missing role, coverage and resulting limit, all derived from the selected assessment; no speculative prose rule. Opaque cursor encodes `(source_time,evidence_id)` and selected basis SHA; mismatch returns 409 rather than changing pages.

## Money schema `0023` and rules

Create four new tables (do not widen the mini-grid `financial_consequence_revision`, whose CHECK constrains `POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST`, `fuel-cost-1`, L and priced amount). The Site FK uses `(tenant_id,site_id)` -> `site(tenant_id,id)`; references to FindingRevision, cold episode revision, assessment revision and accepted evidence use composite `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` keys; the source link uses `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)`. Add scoped unique targets to referenced tables only where `0022` has not already supplied them. All tables have `tenant_id,site_id`; every immutable row has server `created_at`/`finished_at`, with UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers. Downgrade refuses while any of these rows exist; otherwise drops only W2-04 triggers/tables/indexes, including its added scoped unique target on the older link table.

* `cold_consequence_revision`: `id uuid PK`, `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `assessment_revision_id` UUID NOT NULL; `revision_no int >0`, `prior_revision_id uuid NULL`; `kind` CHECK `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`; `state` CHECK `UNPRICED|WITHHELD`; `reason` nonempty; `window_start,window_end timestamptz` with start<end; `room_asset_id uuid`; `overlap_group text`; `quantity_unit` CHECK `degC_min|none`; `quantity_low,quantity_high numeric NULL` nonnegative/ordered; `amount_low,amount_high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`; `finding_basis_sha256,consequence_basis_sha256 char(64)`, `model_id` CHECK `cold-exposure/1|cold-repair/1`; `source_classification`, `source_gate_sha256 char(64)`, `assumption_revision_ids jsonb` array, `limitation text NULL`. Unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,revision_no)`, `(finding_revision_id,kind,consequence_basis_sha256)` and scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for evidence links. DB CHECK requires `amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL` for every row; a later approved migration must widen this. `quantity_unit='none'` requires both quantity bounds null; `degC_min` allows null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row is created for the six replays because no exact accepted cost/work feed exists. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` may carry nonnegative bounded `degC_min`; if insufficient evidence both bounds null. No zero amount or stock count stands for unknown.
* `cold_consequence_evidence`: `consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use,reason` with PK `(consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, `use=SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED` and scoped FKs. Include all exact evidence cited by the pinned Finding/episode basis, no private world keys.
* `cold_consequence_work`: `id uuid PK`, `cause` CHECK `FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED|EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED|SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED`; nullable `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `publication_batch_id`, with a CHECK requiring exactly the locator for its cause, and scoped FKs; `window_start,window_end` (nonempty, half-open), `room_asset_id`, `dirty_key_sha256 char(64)`, `enqueued_at`; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256)` and `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for attempts. Add `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)` uniqueness to `batch_publication_link` in 0023 for the scoped `publication_batch_id` FK; `batch_id` is that table's actual PK, there is no link `id`. Trigger-only INSERT with `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`. A cold FindingRevision or episode revision enqueues its selected Site/window/room; the source-gate trigger enqueues only overlapping heads. No runtime work insert/status update. Backfill existing W2-03 cold heads once during upgrade, idempotently. `0010_financial_assumptions` has a diesel-only DB CHECK as well as a diesel-only publisher: **no `ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED` cause or trigger in 0023**. A future cold assumption contract must migrate the DB, publisher and dirty-work cause together.
* `cold_consequence_work_attempt`: `id uuid PK`, `work_id` scoped FK, `attempt_no int >0`, `outcome` CHECK `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `error_code text NULL`, `consequence_revision_ids jsonb` array, `finished_at`; unique `(work_id,attempt_no)`. Success has no error and exact returned IDs; failure has error and empty IDs. A failed attempt leaves work pending; pending means no successful attempt. Index `(site_id,enqueued_at)` and `(work_id,attempt_no DESC)`.

`overlap_group = 'cold:' + sha256(canonical JSON array [tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id])`: literal lowercase `cold:` prefix, then 64 lowercase hex characters; it is a grouping label, not a V4 event/correlation ID (whose grammar excludes `:`). An excursion and backup Finding of the same selected room/window share it; the financial read groups `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` by this key and takes the single selected excursion exposure basis, never sums both. The start-response Finding may carry a `WITHHELD` context consequence with the same key but zero *counted* exposure entries, not a numeric zero. No cross-window summing without an explicit disjoint-window contract.

The source-gate event is an appended `batch_publication_link` to a previously accepted cold batch, if one exists; its trigger selects only cold heads whose linked accepted IDs use that batch and enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` inside the link transaction. An initial link before acceptance has no heads and creates no money work; later W2-03 Finding/episode append enqueues it. `evidence_publication` is immutable and a new unrelated publication never dirties an existing cold basis. Each trigger derives the same canonical dirty key from `(cause,locator,tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` and takes a Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads, following `0012_evidence_consequence_work.py`'s enqueue lock pattern. The FindingRevision trigger is restricted to `NEW.domain='cold_chain'`. An episode with no warranted Finding still enqueues; the worker records a successful no-op attempt with an empty consequence ID array. No trigger on an accepted disposition bypasses W2-03 assessment: it waits for the new exact cold Finding/episode revision.

`cold-exposure/1` derives observed rectangle degree-minute bounds only from W2-03's persisted episode bounds/accepted evidence, not a continuous curve. A bounded positive or zero technical quantity is allowed only when W2-03 actually proved it; missing-probe/contradictory has null quantity. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is unpriced for sound technical bounds because no `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value`, reviewed `cold_exposure_rule`, product/currency binding or source-gated valuation exists. These are **reserved future scenario keys**, displayed as missing, not publishable via the diesel-price publisher. Product value/currency and EV tariff would be explicitly authored scenario assumptions and still would not prove sales, collections, spoilage or lost revenue. Recorded repair needs an accepted exact cost/currency/work record; none of the six replay cases has one. Mixed/unknown source, closed S4-05 gate, stale/pending assessment, incomplete period or contradictory evidence yields `WITHHELD` and null amount. LR current-money gate also refuses a current money claim from historical/pending basis. `exact_basis` returns selected Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs, accepted evidence UUIDs, source gate digest, assumption revision UUIDs (empty in W2) and consequence basis SHA. Basis SHA hashes canonical sorted IDs, window/cutoff, model/formula version, quantity bounds, source gate and missing assumption markers; an identical basis returns the existing ID and a changed basis appends a new revision.

`PostgresConsequenceStore` alone inserts `cold_consequence_revision`, `cold_consequence_evidence` and `cold_consequence_work_attempt`; add separate cold methods without changing the existing mini-grid methods. `cold_recompute.process_one` selects unfinished work under `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('cold_consequence:' || work_id,0))`, takes one repeatable-read snapshot, refuses a foreign/missing exact basis, writes consequence/links and `SUCCEEDED` attempt in one transaction. On derivation failure it rolls back that transaction and appends a typed `FAILED` attempt in a fresh transaction. A crash before success commits leaves no partial revision; retries are idempotent. `run` uses the existing `financials/recompute.py:retry_due_at` 1,2,4,8,16-minute schedule and bounded batch; one bad work item cannot starve others. Trigger-created work does not depend on a successful worker.

## Five API reads

`cold_site_api.py:build_cold_site_router` mounts five GET paths below `/api/sites/{site_code}/cold`. Request query for all is optional paired `from,to`, optional `known_at` (strict UTC); Analysis additionally `cursor,limit` (default 50, max 200), matching W2-03's paging spelling. Use W2-03 shared common envelope exactly: `site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256},limitations`. Every card/object includes `as_of` and `limitation`; scalar decimals serialize as decimal strings and absent values as JSON null. `currentness.state` is `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. Valid empty selection is 200 with `UNKNOWN` and empty arrays. Use one selected repeatable-read snapshot for W2-03, source disclosure and consequence heads. No previous consequence becomes a current money claim while a relevant cold work item is queued or failed. Errors use `{error:{code,message}}`; never downgrade 503 into a blank page.

Refusal code contract (same scope and parse semantics as `cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`): 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; 403 `SITE_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN` or `COLD_SOURCE_DISCLOSURE_FORBIDDEN`; 404 `SITE_NOT_FOUND`; 409 `COLD_SELECTION_UNSETTLED` for an explicitly requested revision that has no settled exact basis or `COLD_CURSOR_BASIS_CHANGED` for a changed Analysis basis; 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_TOO_LONG`, `COLD_PARAMETER_UNKNOWN`, `COLD_LIMIT_INVALID`, or `COLD_CURSOR_INVALID`; 503 `COLD_READ_UNAVAILABLE`. A wrong-type/foreign Site is 404 after access is checked. An absent optional current basis is 200 `UNKNOWN`, not a 409. Analysis cursor is unpadded base64url of sorted, compact UTF-8 JSON `{basis:<64 lowercase hex>,id:<UUID string>,t:<UTC instant>}`; malformed is 422 and an exact basis mismatch is 409. No arbitrary `after` offset or page across a changed basis.

| GET suffix | Exact additional response |
| --- | --- |
| `/overview` | `status:{state:OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `objective:{text,policy_low_deg_c,policy_high_deg_c,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `temperature_summary:{latest_deg_c,latest_at,coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio},episode_count,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `backup_summary:{attempts,observed_success,observed_failure,unknown,latest_chain_id,as_of,limitation}`, `causal_strip:[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `findings:{count,items:[{finding_id,revision_id,title,claim_level,link}],as_of,limitation}`, `needs_attention:[{code,text,link,as_of,limitation}]`, `consequence_summary:{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|UNKNOWN,quantity:{low,high,unit:'degC_min'|null},amount:{low:null,high:null,currency:null},overlap_group,as_of,limitation}`, `links:{temperature,backup,findings,evidence,analysis,financials}`. The response includes W2-03 selected temperature points only through its linked read, never repackages model truth. |
| `/evidence` | `capabilities:[{claim,state,required_roles,missing_roles,coverage,as_of,limitation}]`, `sources:[{id,role,contact,fact_age,accepted_count,last_accepted_at,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{analysis,foundation,temperature,backup}`. `contact` is `RECENT|STALE|NEVER|UNKNOWN`; `fact_age` is duration/null, never equipment condition. |
| `/evidence/analysis` | `accepted_records:{items:[{evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id,link,as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`, `claim_rules:[{rule_id,requirement,observed_roles,missing_roles,coverage,result,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{evidence,findings}`. No private oracle or rejected ordinal masquerades as accepted. |
| `/financials` | `consequences:[{id,kind,state,quantity_bounds:{low,high},quantity_unit,amount_bounds:{low:null,high:null},currency:null,reason,overlap_group,exact_basis,as_of,limitation}]`, `not_included:[{code:STOCK_VALUE_UNDECLARED|EXPOSURE_RULE_UNAPPROVED|SPOILAGE_UNPROVED|REPAIR_COST_ABSENT|SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD,text,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{assumptions,findings,evidence}`. Return one counted exposure per overlap group; the optional backup context row is marked nonadditive. |
| `/financial-assumptions` | `items:[{key,revision_id,value,unit,currency,valid_from,valid_to,recorded_at,source,as_of,limitation}]`, `missing_keys:['cold_stock_quantity','cold_stock_unit_value','cold_exposure_rule']` in this slice, `publication_state:'NOT_SUPPORTED_IN_W2'`, `links:{financials}`. `items=[]` for six canonical replays. This is a read-only explicit absence statement, not a publication form. |

Links carry the exact selected `from,to,known_at` and record/revision ID through route builders, URL encoded. The Site overview's Finding count counts distinct current Finding IDs, not episode revisions. `needs_attention` is ordered: pending recompute, probe gap, observed start failure, unknown transfer, missing policy; omit a code only when its predicate is false. No â€œnext actionâ€ link unless an actual W2-03 Finding or Evidence destination exists.

## Fixtures, named tests and guards

Use `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/{normal,excursion,backup-failed,missing-probe,late-reading,contradictory}.json` (`cold-replay-manifest/1`) through registered Lab, Stage, gateway Batch V2 and ordinary S2 acceptance; never seed accepted rows. The thirteen required frozen values are room capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min`, control low/high `2/4 degC`, policy low/high `2/4 degC`, minimum excursion `15 min`, return hysteresis `0.2 degC`; probe error `0.1 degC`; compressor cooling `3 kW`, start delay `1 min`; generator start timeout `5 min`. Import/conversion at `2026-10-01T00:00:00Z` is historical v3 compatible; `sites/binding_service.py:BindingService.publish` publishes eleven live v4-mapped channels at `2026-10-01T00:01:00Z` before the run. The six strict `cold-scenario/1` documents start `2026-10-06T19:00:00Z`, end `20:00:00Z`, seed `20417`, model `cold-thermal/1`, publication `cold-publication/1`, observation `cold-observation/1`; the immutable run identity pins Foundation, binding, scenario and observation digests. W2-04 consumes its public accepted IDs and W2-03 exact revision IDs, not the frozen private forcing or run trajectory.

Excursion: first high 19:15, open episode, lower duration 45 min, upper null, one unpriced `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` group. Backup-failed: `OBSERVED_FAILURE`, door CONTEXT, same exposure group counted once. Rule 8: add `cold_consequence_revision,cold_consequence_evidence,cold_consequence_work_attempt` to `$consequenceTables` in `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1` **before** its DML checks; `$consequenceWriter` remains `financials/adapters/postgres_consequence_store.py`. Keep `cold_consequence_work` in its own trigger-only protected set; any runtime `INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE/MERGE/COPY` against it fails, and no runtime `UPDATE/DELETE` against any cold consequence table is allowed. Add per-table cold insert-only checks, not just membership in the existing `$consequenceTables` list. W2-03 adds its episode tables to `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts`. The existing `$pgWriters`, `$pgWritesSeen` and `$pgDriverSeen` already include the two owning adapters: add no new SQL driver import or fake write to a read service; `analytics/cold_site_reads.py`, `cold_site_api.py` and `cold_recompute.py` call injected store ports. If the final W2-03 `ColdReadService` introduces a separate driver-owning adapter, bring its import through an allowed existing adapter or extend a distinct read-only allowlist without treating it as a writer, with non-vacuous proof. Extend `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` with pass probes for each cold consequence/attempt INSERT and migration trigger `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`, and fail probes for foreign writer, runtime work INSERT, UPDATE/DELETE of each table and `DO UPDATE`. The checker fails closed when owner/probe/table registration is absent. Do not loosen ledger message-conflict rules or grant the cold worker accepted-evidence writes.

Dependency direction: pure `financials/cold_consequence.py` and `analytics/cold_site_reads.py` depend on domain values/ports only; FastAPI routers compose them, and SQL stays in `postgres_consequence_store.py` and the existing assessment adapter. No import from financials into the evidence ledger, no simulator/private model import into Site reads, and no HTTP route invokes source designation. Verify these with the repository architecture/workflow checks and a targeted import/probe proof.

Run the registry checker and leave proposed UI/money IDs uncreated pending reviewed scope.

## Final Architect rulings on commit 1 (Q1-Q10)

This section overrides shorthand above where the two differ. Migration `0023` on `task/W2-04-cold-site-evidence-money` is provisional until its listed corrections are made; passing migration tests do not waive them.

1. **Q1 CHANGE â€” selected window and room.** Keep `assessment_revision.window_start/window_end` for each trigger; neither episode bounds nor `window_assessment_revision_id` is a money window. For an `EXCURSION`, `cold_episode.asset_id` is the room. For a `START_RESPONSE`, that asset and the start Finding's `subject_asset_id` are the **generator**, so they must never fill `room_asset_id` or the overlap key. Resolve the effective chamber room from the exact assessment's selected `cold-roles-1` Foundation/binding basis (the same room that its temperature policy names); require one scoped room, or raise and roll back. Apply this to the Finding trigger, episode trigger, source-gate trigger and backfill. Prove that excursion and start Finding for one assessment produce the same room/window/group and that no generator ID is stored as a room.
2. **Q2 ACCEPT.** Source-gate link dirties latest cold episode heads citing the batch through `cold_episode_evidence` and latest cold Finding heads citing it through `finding_revision_evidence`. Scope every subquery by tenant and Site as well as parent ID; distinct heads may produce distinct work rows but identical selected consequence basis returns the same revision.
3. **Q3 CHANGE â€” preserve instant precision.** Canonical JSON array of strings and lowercase hyphenated UUIDs stand. Use UTC `Z` timestamps with six fractional digits **when microseconds are nonzero**, and whole seconds otherwise. This selected-window key is distinct from `source_envelope.instant_text`, which emits whole seconds for envelope publication; W2-03's `_instant` accepts fractional selected bounds. Reject precision beyond microseconds at the API boundary; never truncate a selected window to seconds when hashing. The six replay windows happen to have whole seconds. SQL and Python golden vectors must match for whole-second and fractional inputs.
4. **Q4 corrected against W2-03 data.** `limitation IS NOT NULL` means a named technical limit, not an arbitrary note. W2-03 can persist both observed rectangle degree-minute bounds while `claim.open=true`, `duration_high=NULL` and both end bounds are null. For every selected open excursion with a proved lower bound, store `quantity_low=episode.degree_minutes_low`, `quantity_high=NULL`, `state=UNPRICED` when no other withholding gate applies, and include `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`. The W2-03 finite observed-segment high must not become a high for total episode exposure. Apply this based on open/end state, not `episode.degree_minutes_high IS NULL`; the canonical excursion replay must carry the limit. For a closed, eligible excursion copy both proved bounds. `WITHHELD` may have either/both null bounds. Null amount/currency remains mandatory in every case.
5. **Q5 ACCEPT each extra.** Keep scoped self-FK, first-revision/prior-link CHECK, kind/model pairing, nonnull scoped room FK on revision and work, and nullable evidence-link reason. Add a same-Finding/kind predecessor check in store logic; FK alone does not prove chain membership. A successful no-Finding episode gets a no-op attempt, not a fabricated Finding or consequence.
6. **Q6 ACCEPT.** The listed `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` columns, including evidence-link `created_at`, are server times. The sole store writer must omit them, and tests must assert they lie in the DB transaction's observed interval. Do not accept client timestamps in store methods. A forcing trigger is unnecessary for this owner-restricted append-only table.
7. **Q7 CHANGE â€” constrain the aggregate.** `source_classification` is exactly `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|MIXED|UNKNOWN`, the values returned by `evidence/source_provenance.py`; add a SQL CHECK. `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` is not a current class. Preserve reason/detail and exact IDs separately; `UNKNOWN` and `MIXED` withhold money.
8. **Q8 ACCEPT.** Missing room or invalid assessment window is an invariant violation: raise and roll back the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, following `0012` rather than silently dropping dirty work. The worker reports `FAILED` for later derivation faults; it cannot repair a missing enqueue.
9. **Q9 ACCEPT with a nonvacuous guard.** Rule 8 scans runtime modules, so inspect the exact `0023` migration separately. Require at least one `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work`, and require every such INSERT to use `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`; reject `DO UPDATE`, another arbiter or a missing INSERT. Probe by mutating a temporary copy of the migration and restoring it, with pass and fail cases; no probe may pass solely because migrations are skipped by the runtime scan.
10. **Q10 ACCEPT staged enforcement.** Commit 1 installs table/owner/probe-name registration and refuses foreign and mutating writes. Commit 2 adds real store INSERTs and enables per-table `inserts seen` assertions for revision, evidence and attempt. Before commit 2 is declared done, all three must be nonvacuously seen; no empty/fake SQL is allowed to satisfy the guard. The trigger-only work check is active in commit 1.

## Commit-2 Architect rulings: stored codes and screen copy

These exact codes are approved with the meanings below. They are stable machine values, not user-facing text. The selected `/financials` response and overview money summary render the stated plain-language wording. Translate each code in the semicolon-joined stored `limitation` before display; never show that raw string to users. A limitation is independent of `state`: an open episode can be `UNPRICED` with an unknown upper bound. When multiple limits apply, present each distinct applicable sentence in a stable order; a valuation reason must not hide a technical or source limit.

| Code | Ruling and exact predicate | Plain screen wording |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `COLD_VALUATION_NOT_APPROVED` (reason) | Confirm for an eligible `UNPRICED` exposure; no approved cold stock value, currency or exposure valuation rule. This remains the reason for an open excursion with a proved lower bound. | `Thermal exposure is recorded, but no approved product valuation is available.` |
| `SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD` (reason) | Confirm for `WITHHELD` when the exact source disclosure/provenance gate is closed, mixed or unknown. Do not use for a merely open episode. This takes precedence over the valuation reason. | `The source record cannot support a money claim for this selection.` |
| `TECHNICAL_CLAIM_LIMITED` (reason) | Confirm for `WITHHELD` when the selected episode cannot prove an eligible exposure lower bound because of a technical claim limit, including contradictory evidence, claim level `POSSIBLE`/`UNKNOWN`, or coverage below 0.8. Use when the source gate passes; an unknown upper bound alone does not trigger it. | `The available temperature evidence cannot establish an exposure amount.` |
| `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` (limitation) | Confirm whenever `claim.open=true` on a selected eligible excursion. Set consequence `quantity_high=NULL` even if W2-03's observed-segment high is finite. Keep that observed high in the W2-03 technical read. | `The excursion has no recorded end, so its total exposure has no upper bound.` |
| `CONTRADICTORY_EVIDENCE` (limitation) | Confirm only when W2-03 marks the selected claim contradictory. Do not infer failure. | `Accepted records disagree; this conclusion cannot be established.` |
| `CLAIM_LEVEL_POSSIBLE`, `CLAIM_LEVEL_UNKNOWN` (limitations) | Confirm these two fixed expansions of `CLAIM_LEVEL_<level>` when W2-03 selects that level; do not emit `CLAIM_LEVEL_OBSERVED`, `CLAIM_LEVEL_BOUNDED` or a dynamic/unrecognised suffix. `CONTRADICTORY` uses `CONTRADICTORY_EVIDENCE`. | `The evidence suggests an excursion but does not confirm it.` / `The evidence cannot establish whether an excursion occurred.` |
| `COVERAGE_INCOMPLETE` (limitation) | Confirm only when selected qualifying probe coverage is below 0.8; show the actual known/expected points alongside it. A separate >10-minute gap remains a W2-03 gap limit even if coverage is >=0.8. | `Too few qualifying temperature readings were recorded for an exposure conclusion.` |
| `EXPOSURE_LOWER_BOUND_UNKNOWN` (limitation) | Confirm when no defensible `degC_min` lower bound exists; store `quantity_low=NULL`, never substitute zero. | `The recorded readings do not establish a minimum thermal exposure.` |
| `SOURCE_CLASS_MIXED`, `SOURCE_CLASS_UNKNOWN` (limitations) | Confirm these two fixed expansions of `SOURCE_CLASS_<class>` only for those selected aggregate classes. Do not emit an arbitrary class suffix or treat `REGISTERED_SIMULATION`/`AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` as a technical limitation. | `This selection mixes source types, so its money claim is withheld.` / `The source type is unknown, so its money claim is withheld.` |
| `COLD_RECOMPUTE_FAILED` (worker error) | Confirm as fallback for an unexpected derivation failure after a work item is scoped; retain diagnostic detail in internal logs. Do not mask specific basis/store errors. | If exposed as pending status: `The exposure calculation failed and is waiting to retry.` |
| `STORE_UNAVAILABLE` (worker error) | Confirm for an unavailable consequence/assessment store, matching existing worker vocabulary. A failure to persist a failed attempt cannot be represented as a stored attempt. | If exposed: `The exposure record is temporarily unavailable.` |
| `WORK_SCOPE_MISMATCH` (worker error) | Confirm for a foreign or mismatched work locator. Refuse before derivation and record no attempt against foreign work. | If exposed: `This exposure work item does not belong to the selected site.` |

`COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH` remains the already approved specific worker code. On the six replays, the open excursion retains W2-03's observed rectangle pair in the technical read but produces a consequence with a proved low, null high and `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`; the normal replay has no exposure row. Assert these separately so a copied finite high cannot pass the consequence test.

## Implementer dry-read: exact backend behavior

| Question a builder would otherwise decide | Binding answer and proof |
| --- | --- |
| Which Finding gets an exposure revision? | Only a selected, warranted `EXCURSION` FindingRevision with an exact `claim.episode_revision_id` and eligible episode. `START_RESPONSE` is chronology/context; do not emit the optional context consequence in W2-04. A successful start, unsupported excursion, or missing-probe episode without a Finding produces no consequence and may close its trigger work with an empty-ID success attempt. This avoids a second counted exposure and an invented zero row. |
| Which assessment is exact? | Join FindingRevision `assessment_revision_id`, `claim.episode_revision_id`, episode revision `assessment_revision_id`, and the selected W2-03 assessment pair by scoped IDs. A mismatch is a typed worker failure `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH`, never a latest-row fallback. For a source-gate event, re-read the latest settled heads at its transaction snapshot and enqueue only heads whose exact evidence IDs use the linked batch. |
| How is `degC_min` computed? | For a closed eligible episode, copy the selected excursion revision's `degree_minutes_low/high` as decimal strings. For an open eligible episode, copy the proved low and set the consequence high to null regardless of W2-03's finite observed-segment high; include `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`. Check policy, coverage and evidence eligibility first. Do not integrate an interpolated chart, derive from duration alone or use private trajectory. `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` has no exposure row; no episode means `UNKNOWN` summary if the selection is otherwise incomplete. |
| How does revision identity work? | Hash the sorted exact accepted IDs with their SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED uses and reasons, Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs and basis hashes, selected window/cutoff, `cold-exposure/1`, quantity bounds, aggregate source class/gate digest, and explicit missing cold assumption markers. Existing identical `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)` returns its ID. A changed basis appends `revision_no=max+1` and `prior_revision_id` under the work advisory lock. Never overwrite; reversion to a previously used basis must remain audit-visible, so include the newly selected cutoff/revision ID in the hash. |
| What exactly is pending and when does a work item finish? | Unfinished means no `SUCCEEDED` attempt. Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |
| Which source/cutoff gates apply? | Resolve S4-05 aggregate provenance from the exact evidence IDs, not Site mode or a Lab manifest. Recompute after a new publication link. Return historical technical rows at an older explicit `known_at` with the historical label; never call their amount current. Closed disclosure gives 403 before returning restricted accepted IDs; mixed/unknown classification and pending/stale currentness withhold a current money claim. No cold price exists even with coherent simulation. |
| What does `RECORDED_REPAIR` mean here? | It is schema reserved only. Neither a W2-03 start Finding nor mock WR-019 is an accepted, costed work record. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row, repair amount, currency, incident or maintenance item is synthesized in W2-04. |
| How does the API keep one snapshot? | Authenticate and scope Site first. Open one repeatable-read transaction; capture latest committed S2 disposition watermark once, resolve paired window and optional `known_at`, then call W2-03 read/store, Foundation, source provenance and consequence read methods with the same connection. If W2-03 exposes only HTTP calls, add a caller-connection read method to its existing assessment adapter; never invoke loopback HTTP for composition or import a SQL driver into `cold_site_reads.py`. |
| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. For an unproved cold record or assessment target, display the exact ID as labelled text plus `Analysis view not available for this cold record`; Finding and Site Evidence links that pass their own route proof remain clickable. Never turn a mock Action, Incident, Maintenance or unbuilt Cooling destination into a link. |

Use `cold_site_reads.py` as a pure composition service, and put SQL in the existing assessment/consequence adapters. `financials/cold_recompute.py` receives store protocols and no SQL driver. The five GETs are composed in `main.py`; do not change the mini-grid routers. Run architecture/dependency, workflow, writer guard and targeted import probes after each owning commit. The review packet states the exact migration SHA, W2-03 predecessor revision, replay manifest IDs, accepted IDs, selected revision IDs and test database name.

## Final Run-5 Architect rulings for commit 4 (Q-R1â€“Q-R9)

This section governs earlier shorthand and every missing field in the Run-5 handoff. Literal JSON field names, enum values, ordering and user copy below are binding. The named v6.9 slots are in `frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js`: `add('cold',...)`, `SITE_EVIDENCE.cold`, `EVIDENCE_RECORDS.cold`, `SITE_FIN.cold`, and `siteAssumptionsScreen('cold')`. | Question | Ruling, exact response and v6.9 slot |
| --- | --- |
| Q-R1 | `/overview.needs_attention` is always an array of `{code,text,link,as_of,limitation}` in this order. Codes and text are `COLD_RECOMPUTE_PENDING` â†’ `New evidence is waiting to be assessed`, `COLD_PROBE_GAP` â†’ `Chamber temperature has an unmeasured gap`, `COLD_START_FAILURE` â†’ `Automatic generator start failed in the accepted record`, `COLD_TRANSFER_UNKNOWN` â†’ `Transfer-switch position was not observed`, `COLD_POLICY_MISSING` â†’ `Chamber temperature policy is not declared for this window`. Pending is selected currentness or unfinished overlapping work; probe gap is a selected W2-03 gap; start failure is an `OBSERVED_FAILURE` chain; unknown transfer needs a selected backup chain lacking an observed transfer step; missing policy needs absent/unresolved selected bounds. For each code, first use the selected Finding URL for that item's analytic when such a Finding exists. Otherwise `COLD_START_FAILURE` and `COLD_TRANSFER_UNKNOWN` use the selected backup-chain read URL; `COLD_RECOMPUTE_PENDING`, `COLD_PROBE_GAP` and `COLD_POLICY_MISSING` use the selected Evidence read URL. If that exact selected destination is unavailable, use `null`; never an Action URL or an unrelated Finding. `as_of` is the selected analytic revision time or cutoff for a pending item. For `COLD_POLICY_MISSING`, `limitation` is the unresolved-policy sentence when the selected policy revision is unresolved; otherwise it is the selected excursion assessment's limitation. For every other code, `limitation` is its exact selected limitation or `null`. This fills `cold` panel 3 ranked attention. Omit false predicates, never emit a generic invented work row. |
| Q-R2 | Add `/overview.cooling:{state:'SUPPORTED'|'LIMITED'|'UNAVAILABLE'|'CONTRADICTORY',text,as_of,limitation}` and `/overview.maintenance:{state:'NOT_RECORDED',text:'No maintenance work record is available for this window',record_id:null,link:null,as_of,limitation}`. ### Reading verdicts (Run 5)

1. **CORRECT capability details; ACCEPT the state scale.** `TEMPERATURE_POINT` needs at least one *qualifying* accepted calibrated probe point with attested clock, not merely any known point. `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` needs selected policy, at least two qualifying points, ratio `>=0.8` and no bridge `>10 minutes`. `BACKUP_START_RESULT` needs a correlated observed success/failure chain; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own observed transfer step; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs accepted compressor state and at least two qualifying points; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` needs an eligible OBSERVED/BOUNDED excursion with an exposure Finding **and proved thermal bounds**, not stock loss. Partial evidence is LIMITED only where it supports a narrower claim; absent prerequisites are UNAVAILABLE; contradictory selected W2-03 facts/steps are CONTRADICTORY. `required_roles` use `REQUIRED_ROLES` (the two policy keys are declared policy prerequisites, not source channels); event claim coverage for BACKUP_START_RESULT and TRANSFER_POSITION is `{known_points:null,expected_points:null,ratio:null}`. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 1 and Analysis panel 2. A missing clock/calibration must not produce SUPPORTED from a numeric point count.
2. **ACCEPT backup chain selection** with deterministic tie handling: choose the selected chain with greatest `(anchor_time,correlation_id)`; `latest_chain_id` is its correlation ID and `causal_strip` contains only that chain's ordered steps. If no chain, ID is null and strip is empty. This fills `cold` panel 1.
3. **ACCEPT last accepted fact time.** `/evidence.sources[].last_accepted_at` is the maximum accepted `source_time` for that `(source_id,role)` within the half-open window and cutoff, never `decided_at`; `fact_age` is `max(0,to-last_accepted_at)` whole seconds or null. Label it `Last accepted fact`, separate from `Contact checked at window end {to}`. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 2.
4. **ACCEPT source row identity/order.** Return selected runs' bound `(source_id,role)` channels, deduplicated and sorted by `cold-roles-1` order, then role and source UUID. The mock's six grouped display rows remain in their order; the inverter/battery display row may contain multiple distinct channel details, with no fabricated combined observed device. A configured but unbound mock slot says `No source-specific record`; `Not connected` requires configuration proof. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 2.
5. **CORRECT five UNKNOWN-contact sentences** to exact plain copy: `CONTACT_NO_DIAGNOSTIC` â†’ `No gateway diagnostic was available by the window end and evidence cutoff.`; `CONTACT_NOT_REPORTED` â†’ `The selected gateway diagnostic has no contact time for this source.`; `CONTACT_NO_CADENCE` â†’ `The gateway's reporting interval is not declared, so source contact cannot be called recent.`; `CONTACT_CLOCK` â†’ `The gateway time cannot be verified, so the reported contact time is uncertain.`; `CONTACT_AFTER_WINDOW` â†’ `The reported source contact happened after this window ended.` Use exactly one as `sources[].limitation` with `contact:'UNKNOWN',contact_at:null`. Explicit source entry `null` is `NEVER`, `contact_at:null`, with text `No source contact recorded`; no diagnostic/read failure is not NEVER. `RECENT` includes equality at `2 Ã— declared cadence`; `STALE` is later. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 2 Status and its detail.
6. **ACCEPT pending money; CORRECT group selection.** Current money is WITHHELD if assessment currentness is PENDING_RECOMPUTE or overlapping consequence work has no SUCCEEDED attempt; reason `New evidence is waiting to be assessed`, or the existing `SCREEN_WORDING['COLD_RECOMPUTE_FAILED']` after a failed latest attempt. Within each overlap group, take one PRODUCT_EXPOSURE head from the exact selected Finding revisions, ordered by `(created_at,id)` descending after the highest `revision_no` per Finding/kind; do not add a backup context row to the exposure count. If multiple equally eligible exposure heads represent different Findings in one group, selecting the latest is display deduplication only; retain their separate exact bases in storage. This fills `cold` panel 4 and `cold-financials` panel 1.
7. **ACCEPT cutoff handling with a condition.** `cold_heads_on` is keyed by the exact FindingRevision selected at the cutoff; do not filter its consequence by `created_at<=known_at`, because derivation may finish later. An uncompleted or failed consequence job still withholds money. Analysis record links stay UNAVAILABLE until Q-R9 proof; existing null link and exact limitation are correct. This fills the historical money and Analysis states.

Reject submicrosecond precision independently. The selected `known_at` remains visible in the date bar, including pending/historical reads, on all five routes.

## Implementer readings to confirm (spec does not name these values; no design was intended)

1. **Q4 vs. data.** W2-03 stores both `degree_minutes_low` and `degree_minutes_high` for the open excursion
   (`claim.open = true`; duration upper is null). As the dry-read says to copy the bounds, the row has both, and
   `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` is set only when the upper bound is null. So none of the six replays carries that
   limit; it is covered by a pure test. Architect question: should an open episode carry
   `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` (or a null upper bound) even when W2-03 gives a degree-minute upper bound?
2. **Stored codes.** Reasons are `COLD_VALUATION_NOT_APPROVED` (UNPRICED), `SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD` and
   `TECHNICAL_CLAIM_LIMITED`. Limitation codes are `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY_EVIDENCE`,
   `CLAIM_LEVEL_<level>`, `COVERAGE_INCOMPLETE` (below 0.8, the floor in the spec's EXCURSION_BOUNDS rule),
   `EXPOSURE_LOWER_BOUND_UNKNOWN` and `SOURCE_CLASS_<class>`; several are joined with "; ".
3. **Withholding.**
   - A technical limit nulls the quantity.
   - Withholding for the source alone keeps the proved bounds.
   - Currentness (pending or historical) is applied at read time in commit 3, not stored.
4. **Worker error codes.** `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH` (from the spec). The others are this run's choice:
   - `COLD_RECOMPUTE_FAILED` for any other fault;
   - `STORE_UNAVAILABLE`;
   - `WORK_SCOPE_MISMATCH` for foreign work, which records no attempt.
5. **Race.** The RR snapshot starts at the lock statement, so a racing loser hits a unique constraint. It then
   records nothing, because `cold_record_failure` sees the success, and reports `already_succeeded`. The test proves
   one attempt and one revision.

  commit 1.

export type GapId = keyof typeof UI_GAP_CATALOGUE;
export type GapPlacement<G extends GapId> = (typeof UI_GAP_CATALOGUE)[G]["placements"][number];

/** True only for a known gap ID and one of its registered placements. */
export function isKnownGapPlacement(gapId: string, placement: string): boolean {
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(UI_GAP_CATALOGUE, gapId)) return false;
  const placements: readonly string[] = UI_GAP_CATALOGUE[gapId as GapId].placements;
  return placements.includes(placement);
}

// Missing-claim orphan scan (G-01, D-2026-10-12-mock-gap-closure-mechanism section 2).
//
// Product source only (frontend/src, excluding tests and test helpers). It enforces:
//   1. the typed UI mirror (frontend/src/ui/gapCatalogue.ts) equals the registry mock_gaps placements
//      that the UI may reference (open with placements, or resolved with a reviewed ui_transition);
//   2. every <MissingClaim gapId placement> uses literal, catalogued pairs, and every catalogued placement is used;
//   3. missing-capability copy ("not available yet" and equivalents) is either rendered through <MissingClaim>
//      or listed in the dated legacy baseline, which must match exactly and may only shrink.
//
// Run: node tools/checks/missing-claim-scan.mjs   (exit 1 with file:line on failure)
// Do not weaken these checks to make a task pass; shrink the baseline by tagging copy instead.
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const repoRoot = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../..');
const require = createRequire(path.join(repoRoot, 'frontend/package.json'));
const ts = require('typescript');

export const BASELINE_PATH = 'tools/checks/missing-claim-baseline.json';
export const REGISTRY_PATH = 'frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json';
export const MIRROR_PATH = 'frontend/src/ui/gapCatalogue.ts';
export const SCAN_ROOT = 'frontend/src';
// Frozen at the dated G-01 seed. The baseline total can never exceed it, even on a branch whose
// merge-base baseline is missing. Lowering it is allowed; raising it is a guard change for review.
export const SEED_CEILING = 16;
// Missing-capability phrasing. Runtime zero/error copy ("unavailable", "not recorded", "no results") is not
// matched: those are states of an existing capability, not catalogued gaps.
// G-02 (Architect C7, D-2026-10-13-g01-placement-and-orphan-scan-boundary) adds two reviewed specific cues:
// "not tracked yet" and the commissioning-specific "commissioning confirmation not recorded". Generic
// "not recorded", "unavailable" and "no results" stay out. A new euphemism needs a reviewed cue here (with a
// failing mutation fixture), never a silent baseline entry.
export const PATTERNS = [
  /not available yet/i,
  /not yet available/i,
  /no reviewed source/i,
  /not tracked yet/i,
  /commissioning confirmation not recorded/i,
  // G-02 Q2 (Architect): exact/scoped cues only. "not assessed", "not declared" and "No ... record here" are
  // NOT matched globally: they also describe ordinary selected-window, configuration or runtime states.
  /(?<!dispatch )not\s+available\s+here/i, // "Dispatch not available here" is a component-integration state (Q6)
  /\breadings?\b[^.]{0,40}\bnot\s+assessed\b/i, // SLD: "Readings not assessed here", "any reading has arrived is not assessed"
  /not\s+assessed\s+on\s+this\s+diagram/i,
  /no\s+proposed-change\s+record\s+here/i,
  /no\s+linked\s+constraint\s+evidence/i,
];

// Reviewed placement exemptions (W1-UX-07, D-2026-10-17-w1-rail). Each names one file, the one const that
// holds the copy, and its exact text. The hit is exempt only when that const initialiser is the literal and
// every reference to the const is in the same file, so the phrase cannot leak onto a product panel through an
// import. The same phrase anywhere else, or in that file outside the const, is still untagged copy. Adding an
// entry is a guard change for review; this is not a general suppression of the phrase.

const toPosix = (p) => p.split(path.sep).join('/');
const normalize = (text) => text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 160);

export function isProductSource(relPath) {
  const p = toPosix(relPath);
  if (!p.startsWith(`${SCAN_ROOT}/`) || !/\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(p) || p.endsWith('.d.ts')) return false;
  if (/(^|\/)__tests__\//.test(p) || /\.test\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(p) || p.startsWith(`${SCAN_ROOT}/test/`)) return false;
  return true;
}

const parse = (file) => ts.createSourceFile(file.path, file.text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true,
  file.path.endsWith('.tsx') ? ts.ScriptKind.TSX : ts.ScriptKind.TS);
const lineOf = (sf, node) => sf.getLineAndCharacterOfPosition(node.getStart(sf)).line + 1;
const tagName = (node) => (ts.isJsxElement(node) ? node.openingElement.tagName : ts.isJsxSelfClosingElement(node) ? node.tagName : null);
// MissingClaim renders HTML; MissingClaimSvg renders an SVG <text>/<tspan>. Both carry the same checked pair.
const isMissingClaimElement = (node) => ['MissingClaim', 'MissingClaimSvg'].includes(tagName(node)?.getText());
function insideMissingClaim(node) {
  for (let cur = node.parent; cur; cur = cur.parent) if (ts.isJsxElement(cur) && isMissingClaimElement(cur)) return true;
  return false;
}
function stringParts(node) {
  if (ts.isStringLiteral(node) || ts.isNoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral(node)) return node.text;
  if (ts.isTemplateHead(node) || ts.isTemplateMiddle(node) || ts.isTemplateTail(node)) return node.text;
  if (ts.isJsxText(node)) return node.text;
  return null;
}
// The const a literal initialises, through parentheses, concatenation and template spans.
function owningConst(node) {
  let cur = node;
  while (cur.parent && (ts.isParenthesizedExpression(cur.parent) || ts.isBinaryExpression(cur.parent) ||
    ts.isTemplateSpan(cur.parent) || ts.isTemplateExpression(cur.parent) || ts.isAsExpression(cur.parent))) cur = cur.parent;
  const decl = cur.parent;
  if (decl && ts.isVariableDeclaration(decl) && decl.initializer === cur && ts.isIdentifier(decl.name) &&
      decl.parent && (decl.parent.flags & ts.NodeFlags.Const)) return decl.name.text;
  return null;
}

/**
 * Pure analysis. files: [{path, text}] product sources (repo-relative, posix); registry: parsed registry JSON;
 * mirror: {path, text}; baseline: parsed baseline JSON; mainBaseline: parsed merge-base baseline or null.
 * Returns {errors: [{type, where, reason}], untagged: [{file, line, text}], usages: [{file, line, gapId, placement}]}.
 */
export function analyze({ files, registry, mirror, baseline, mainBaseline = null, seedCeiling = SEED_CEILING, exemptions = REVIEWED_EXEMPTIONS }) {
  const errors = [];
  const err = (type, where, reason) => errors.push({ type, where, reason });

  // ---- 1. mirror equals the registry's UI-referable placements ----
  const expected = new Map();
  for (const gap of registry?.mock_gaps?.gaps || []) {
    if (!Array.isArray(gap.ui_placements) || !gap.ui_placements.length) continue;
    if (gap.status === 'open') expected.set(gap.id, { status: 'open', placements: [...gap.ui_placements].sort() });
    else if (gap.status === 'resolved' && gap.evidence?.ui_transition) expected.set(gap.id, { status: 'resolved_transition', placements: [...gap.ui_placements].sort() });
  }
  const mirrorMap = new Map();
  const msf = parse(mirror);
  let mirrorFound = false;
  const visitMirror = (node) => {
    if (ts.isVariableDeclaration(node) && ts.isIdentifier(node.name) && node.name.text === 'UI_GAP_CATALOGUE' && node.initializer) {
      mirrorFound = true;
      let init = node.initializer;
      while (ts.isAsExpression(init) || ts.isSatisfiesExpression(init) || ts.isParenthesizedExpression(init)) init = init.expression;
      if (!ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(init)) { err('mirror', MIRROR_PATH, 'UI_GAP_CATALOGUE must be an object literal'); return; }
      for (const prop of init.properties) {
        const id = prop.name && (ts.isStringLiteral(prop.name) || ts.isIdentifier(prop.name)) ? prop.name.text : null;
        const value = ts.isPropertyAssignment(prop) ? prop.initializer : null;
        if (!id || !value || !ts.isObjectLiteralExpression(value)) { err('mirror', `${MIRROR_PATH}:${lineOf(msf, prop)}`, 'each entry must be a literal {status, placements}'); continue; }
        const field = (name) => value.properties.find((p) => ts.isPropertyAssignment(p) && p.name.getText(msf) === name)?.initializer;
        const status = field('status');
        const placements = field('placements');
        if (!status || !ts.isStringLiteral(status) || !placements || !ts.isArrayLiteralExpression(placements) ||
            placements.elements.some((e) => !ts.isStringLiteral(e))) {
          err('mirror', `${MIRROR_PATH}:${lineOf(msf, prop)}`, `${id}: status and placements must be string literals`); continue;
        }
        if (mirrorMap.has(id)) err('mirror', `${MIRROR_PATH}:${lineOf(msf, prop)}`, `${id}: duplicate mirror entry`);
        mirrorMap.set(id, { status: status.text, placements: placements.elements.map((e) => e.text).sort() });
      }
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node, visitMirror);
  };
  visitMirror(msf);
  if (!mirrorFound) err('mirror', MIRROR_PATH, 'UI_GAP_CATALOGUE not found');
  for (const [id, entry] of mirrorMap) {
    const want = expected.get(id);
    if (!want) err('mirror_unknown', MIRROR_PATH, `${id} is not an open (or reviewed resolved-transition) registry gap with ui_placements`);
    else if (JSON.stringify(want) !== JSON.stringify(entry)) err('mirror_mismatch', MIRROR_PATH, `${id}: mirror ${JSON.stringify(entry)} differs from registry ${JSON.stringify(want)}`);
  }
  for (const id of expected.keys()) if (!mirrorMap.has(id)) err('mirror_missing', MIRROR_PATH, `${id} has registry ui_placements but no UI mirror entry`);

  // ---- 2 and 3. product source scan ----
  const usages = [];
  const literalHits = [];
  const constRefs = new Map(); // const name -> [{inside, file}]
  for (const file of files) {
    const sf = parse(file);
    const visit = (node) => {
      if ((ts.isJsxElement(node) || ts.isJsxSelfClosingElement(node)) && isMissingClaimElement(node)) {
        const opening = ts.isJsxElement(node) ? node.openingElement : node;
        const attr = (name) => opening.attributes.properties.find((a) => ts.isJsxAttribute(a) && a.name.getText(sf) === name);
        const literal = (a) => (a?.initializer && ts.isStringLiteral(a.initializer) ? a.initializer.text : null);
        const where = `${file.path}:${lineOf(sf, node)}`;
        const gapId = literal(attr('gapId'));
        const placement = literal(attr('placement'));
        if (opening.attributes.properties.some((a) => ts.isJsxSpreadAttribute(a)) || gapId === null || placement === null) {
          err('ui_usage', where, 'MissingClaim needs literal gapId and placement attributes (no spread or expression)');
        } else {
          const tag = tagName(node).getText();
          const asValue = literal(attr('as'));
          if (tag === 'MissingClaimSvg' && asValue !== null && !['text', 'tspan'].includes(asValue)) err('ui_usage', where, 'MissingClaimSvg as must be "text" or "tspan" (never an HTML element inside SVG)');
          else if (tag === 'MissingClaimSvg' && attr('as') && asValue === null) err('ui_usage', where, 'MissingClaimSvg as must be a literal "text" or "tspan"');
          if (tag === 'MissingClaim' && (asValue === 'text' || asValue === 'tspan')) err('ui_usage', where, 'MissingClaim renders HTML; use MissingClaimSvg for SVG text');
          usages.push({ file: file.path, line: lineOf(sf, node), gapId, placement });
          const entry = mirrorMap.get(gapId);
          if (!entry) err('ui_unknown_gap', where, `unknown gap ID ${gapId}; register it in mock_gaps and the UI mirror`);
          else if (!entry.placements.includes(placement)) err('ui_unknown_placement', where, `${placement} is not a registered placement of ${gapId}`);
        }
      }
      if (ts.isIdentifier(node) && node.parent && !ts.isImportSpecifier(node.parent) && !ts.isExportSpecifier(node.parent) &&
          !(ts.isVariableDeclaration(node.parent) && node.parent.name === node) && !ts.isImportClause(node.parent)) {
        if (!constRefs.has(node.text)) constRefs.set(node.text, []);
        constRefs.get(node.text).push({ inside: insideMissingClaim(node), file: file.path });
      }
      const text = stringParts(node);
      if (text !== null && PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(text))) {
        literalHits.push({ file: file.path, line: lineOf(sf, node), text: normalize(text), inside: insideMissingClaim(node), constName: owningConst(node) });
      }
      ts.forEachChild(node, visit);
    };
    visit(sf);
  }
  const used = new Set();
  const firstUse = new Map();
  for (const u of usages) {
    const k = `${u.gapId}\u0000${u.placement}`;
    used.add(k);
    if (firstUse.has(k)) {
      const first = firstUse.get(k);
      err('ui_duplicate_placement', `${u.file}:${u.line}`, `${u.gapId} placement ${u.placement} is already used at ${first.file}:${first.line}; a placement is rendered once, register another placement for a new claim`);
    } else firstUse.set(k, u);
  }
  for (const [id, entry] of mirrorMap) for (const placement of entry.placements) {
    if (!used.has(`${id}\u0000${placement}`)) err('unused_placement', MIRROR_PATH, `${id} placement ${placement} is never rendered; place it or give the gap an unplaced_reason`);
  }
  const isExempt = (hit) => exemptions.some((x) => x.file === hit.file && x.constName === hit.constName &&
    x.text === hit.text && (constRefs.get(hit.constName) || []).every((r) => r.file === x.file));
  const untagged = literalHits.filter((hit) => {
    if (hit.inside) return false;
    if (isExempt(hit)) return false;
    if (!hit.constName) return true;
    const refs = constRefs.get(hit.constName) || [];
    return !(refs.length > 0 && refs.every((r) => r.inside));
  }).map(({ file, line, text }) => ({ file, line, text }));

  // ---- baseline: exact, dated, enumerated, shrink-only ----
  const keyOf = (e) => `${e.file}\u0000${e.text}`;
  const current = new Map();
  for (const hit of untagged) {
    const k = keyOf(hit);
    if (!current.has(k)) current.set(k, { file: hit.file, text: hit.text, lines: [] });
    current.get(k).lines.push(hit.line);
  }
  const entries = Array.isArray(baseline?.entries) ? baseline.entries : null;
  if (!baseline || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(baseline.dated || '') || !baseline.scope || !baseline.reason || !entries) {
    err('baseline_shape', BASELINE_PATH, 'baseline needs dated (YYYY-MM-DD), scope, reason and entries');
  }
  const base = new Map();
  for (const entry of entries || []) {
    if (!entry || typeof entry.file !== 'string' || typeof entry.text !== 'string' || !Number.isInteger(entry.count) || entry.count < 1 || !entry.reason) {
      err('baseline_shape', BASELINE_PATH, `entry ${JSON.stringify(entry)} needs file, text, positive integer count and reason`); continue;
    }

exec

const path = require('node:path');
const {validateMockGaps, summarizeMockGaps} = require('./mock_gaps.cjs');

const gap = (id, extra = {}) => ({id, screen_ids: ['readiness'], panel_portion_ids: [], element_ids: ['readiness:2.firmware'],
  claim: 'c', boundary: {window: 'w', subject: 's', source: 'none', cannot_mean: 'n'}, missing_kind: 'data_source',
  status: 'open', target_wave: 'unplanned', allocation: 'pending owner review', requires: [], source_status: 'absent',
  ui_placements: [`readiness.${id.slice('G-readiness-'.length)}`], unplaced_reason: null, owner_or_role: 'Owner',
  evidence: {source: null, ui: null, visual: null, reviewed_by: null}, review_trigger: 't', ...extra});

function world(gaps = [gap('G-readiness-firmware')], extra = {}) {
  const registry = {
    nodes: [
      {id: 'A-root', kind: 'architecture', status: 'resolved', requires: []},

        {key: 'sld:1', claim_portions: [{id: 'sld:1#a', core: false, state: 'delivered'}]},
      ],
    },
    mock_gaps: {version: 1, gap_enrolled_screens: [], gaps},
    ...extra,
  };
  const contracts = [['readiness', 1], ['readiness', 2], ['sld', 1], ['other', 1]].map(([screen_id, panel_index]) => ({screen_id, panel_index}));
  const screenLedger = [{id: 'readiness'}, {id: 'sld'}, {id: 'other'}];
  return {registry, opts: {contracts, screenLedger, screenIds: screenLedger.map((r) => r.id), requirePresent: true}};

const errorsOf = (mutate) => { const w = world(); mutate(w); return validateMockGaps(w.registry, w.opts); };
const hasError = (errors, type, pattern) => errors.some((e) => e.type === type && pattern.test(e.reason));

test('the real registry catalogue and ledger joins are valid', () => {
  const read = (name) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, name), 'utf8'));
  const registry = read('v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json');
  const screenLedger = read('v69-screen-ledger.json').screens;
  const errors = validateMockGaps(registry, {contracts: read('v69-panel-contracts.json').panels, screenLedger,
    screenIds: screenLedger.map((r) => r.id), requirePresent: true});
  assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
  const firmware = registry.mock_gaps.gaps.find((g) => g.id === 'G-readiness-gateway-firmware');
  assert.deepEqual(firmware.ui_placements, ['readiness.gateway.firmware']);
  assert.equal(firmware.status, 'open');
  assert.equal(firmware.source_status, 'absent');
  // G-02 enrolls the V-01a four; their ledger rows list exactly the gaps naming each screen.
  const four = ['sites', 'foundation', 'readiness', 'sld'];
  assert.deepEqual(registry.mock_gaps.gap_enrolled_screens, four);
  for (const screen of four) {
    const expected = registry.mock_gaps.gaps.filter((g) => g.screen_ids.includes(screen)).map((g) => g.id).sort();
    assert.ok(expected.length > 0, `${screen} has catalogued gaps`);
    assert.deepEqual([...screenLedger.find((r) => r.id === screen).gap_ids].sort(), expected);
  }
  // C5: uptime, completeness and signal strength are three gaps with one placement each, beside firmware.
  for (const claim of ['uptime', 'completeness', 'signal-strength', 'firmware']) {
    const gap = registry.mock_gaps.gaps.find((g) => g.id === `G-readiness-gateway-${claim}`);
    assert.deepEqual(gap.ui_placements, [`readiness.gateway.${claim}`]);
    assert.deepEqual([gap.missing_kind, gap.source_status, gap.target_wave], ['data_source', 'absent', 'unplanned']);
  }
  // Every enrolled non-core undelivered portion names at least one gap.
  for (const row of registry.delivery_plan.w1_panel_mapping.filter((r) => four.includes(r.key.split(':')[0]))) {
    for (const p of row.claim_portions.filter((p) => !p.core && p.state !== 'delivered')) assert.ok(p.gap_ids?.length, p.id);
  }
});

test('valid fixture passes; absent catalogue is an error only when required', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(errorsOf(() => {}), []);
  const w = world();
  delete w.registry.mock_gaps;
  assert.ok(hasError(validateMockGaps(w.registry, w.opts), 'catalogue', /missing/));
  assert.deepEqual(validateMockGaps(w.registry, {...w.opts, requirePresent: false}), []);
  assert.equal(summarizeMockGaps(w.registry).present, false);
});

test('catalogue version and shape', () => {
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.version = 2; }), 'catalogue', /version/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { delete w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].review_trigger; }), 'gap_shape', /review_trigger/));

test('duplicate, malformed, colliding and wrongly prefixed IDs are rejected', () => {
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps.push(gap('G-readiness-firmware', {ui_placements: ['readiness.other']})); }), 'gap_duplicate', /duplicate/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].id = 'G-Readiness_Firmware'; }), 'gap_id', /must match/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.nodes.push({id: 'G-readiness-firmware', kind: 'data', status: 'open', requires: []}); }), 'gap_duplicate', /collides/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].id = 'G-sld-firmware'; }), 'gap_id', /prefix/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].id = 'G-shared-firmware'; w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].ui_placements = ['shared.firmware']; }), 'gap_id', /at least two/));
});

test('unknown screen, portion and element references are rejected', () => {
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].screen_ids.push('nowhere'); }), 'gap_reference', /unknown screen nowhere/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].panel_portion_ids = ['readiness:9#z']; }), 'gap_reference', /unknown panel portion/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].panel_portion_ids = ['sld:1#a']; }), 'gap_reference', /not on a listed screen/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].element_ids = ['readiness:7.firmware']; }), 'gap_reference', /unknown panel/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].element_ids = ['readiness-firmware']; }), 'gap_reference', /must be/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].element_ids = []; }), 'gap_reference', /no panel portion or element/));
});

test('enum and wave/status/source combinations are enforced', () => {
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].missing_kind = 'vibes'; }), 'gap_enum', /missing_kind/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].status = 'closed'; }), 'gap_enum', /status/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].target_wave = 'W9'; }), 'gap_enum', /target_wave/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].allocation = 'later'; }), 'gap_combination', /pending owner review/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].target_wave = 'W2'; }), 'gap_combination', /owner allocation evidence/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].source_status = 'existing'; }), 'gap_combination', /cannot claim an existing source/));
});

test('resolved gaps need an existing source, a planned wave and reviewed source, UI and visual evidence', () => {
  const errors = errorsOf((w) => { Object.assign(w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0], {status: 'resolved'}); });
  assert.ok(hasError(errors, 'gap_combination', /existing source/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errors, 'gap_combination', /cannot be unplanned/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errors, 'gap_combination', /lacks reviewed evidence: source, ui, visual, reviewed_by/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errors, 'gap_combination', /ui_transition/));
  const resolved = errorsOf((w) => {
    Object.assign(w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0], {status: 'resolved', source_status: 'existing', target_wave: 'W1', allocation: 'owner.md',
      requires: ['A-root'], evidence: {source: 's.md', ui: 'u.md', visual: 'v.md', reviewed_by: 'r.md', ui_transition: 't.md'}});
  });
  assert.deepEqual(resolved, []);
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => {
    Object.assign(w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0], {status: 'resolved', source_status: 'existing', target_wave: 'W1', allocation: 'owner.md',
      requires: ['I-open'], evidence: {source: 's.md', ui: 'u.md', visual: 'v.md', reviewed_by: 'r.md', ui_transition: 't.md'}});
  }), 'gap_combination', /requires open node I-open/));
});

    w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps = [gap('G-readiness-a', {requires: ['G-readiness-b']}), gap('G-readiness-b', {requires: ['G-readiness-a']})];
  }), 'gap_cycle', /cycle/));
});

test('open gaps need exactly one of a placement or an unplaced reason; built W1 screens cannot say "not built"', () => {
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].ui_placements = []; }), 'gap_placement', /neither/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].unplaced_reason = 'Pending'; }), 'gap_placement', /both/));

  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].ui_placements = ['sld.firmware']; }), 'gap_placement', /listed screen/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps[0].ui_placements = ['Readiness Firmware']; }), 'gap_placement', /invalid placement/));
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gaps.push(gap('G-readiness-other', {ui_placements: ['readiness.firmware']})); }), 'gap_placement', /already belongs/));
});

test('enrolled screens need gap_ids on every non-core undelivered portion and on their ledger row', () => {

  assert.ok(hasError(errors, 'gap_enrollment', /readiness:2#a has no gap_ids/));
  assert.ok(!errors.some((e) => /readiness:2#b/.test(e.reason)), 'core portions are not enrolled coverage');
  assert.ok(hasError(errors, 'gap_enrollment', /ledger row/));
  assert.deepEqual(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gap_enrolled_screens = ['sld']; w.opts.screenLedger[1].gap_ids = []; }), [],
    'a delivered non-core portion needs no gap');
  assert.ok(hasError(errorsOf((w) => { w.registry.mock_gaps.gap_enrolled_screens = ['nowhere']; }), 'gap_enrollment', /unknown/));
});

Do not render an anchor, `href="#"`, or a disabled link. The Cooling card reads `Recovery comparison not assessed` alone; remove the appended ` · Not available yet`. If that card has a separate null destination slot, use the same `No linked page for this item` fallback there. These are selected runtime states, so do not register a `mock_gaps` entry for them. The existing `UnavailableEntry` owns genuinely unbuilt tabs and keeps its reviewed exemption. Run the orphan scan and its fixtures unchanged; an actual missing cold capability introduced later needs a registered gap in `v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json`, its `gapCatalogue.ts` mirror, matching screen-ledger `gap_ids`, and a `<MissingClaim>` placement. This ruling is limited to the named null destinations and Cooling copy.
+
+**Accepted total and expansion.** Approve `PostgresAssessmentStore.accepted_count_on` and `accepted_records.total_count`: count all accepted public records in the same tenant, Site, half-open window, committed `known_at`, source gate and pinned assessment selection as the page query, before `limit`/cursor, including records whose assessment use is CONTEXT or EXCLUDED. It is a count of records, not a count of pages, claims or unique sources. `Show all {N} accepted records` uses that total even on a later page; the first three remain the closed mock rows, and the existing cursor still fences continuation. Never count a restricted record in a visible total after a 403 source gate. Keep the non-vacuous >7-record pagination assertion and verify `total_count` is unchanged across pages of one basis.

Remove the earlier Run-7 instruction to list exact accepted IDs inside each rule. A later rule-level lineage feature requires a reviewed persisted rule-to-evidence contract.
+

Panel 2 Quality is `No accepted fact` for total zero, `Accepted facts · good quality` for good-only, `Accepted facts · limited quality` for other-only, or `Accepted facts · mixed quality` for both. Missing-role display uses the same role names. Panel-1 What-we-can-say sentences are fixed by state: for Full `Selected records support this claim for the covered window.`; Limited `Selected records support only part of this claim.`; None `This claim is not established from the selected records.`; Contradictory `Selected records contradict one another.` Append `Thermal exposure does not prove product loss.` on the Product exposure row for every state. The panel-1 Next-step cell is `Review evidence` for the first three rows and `Review financials` for Product exposure, using the selected exact link or noninteractive `Not available yet`. The Overview event-strip prose is fixed as `The steps below are based on the selected accepted records.
